This paper investigates whether LiDAR can reduce this search overhead by providing a surface-aware prior without assuming a direct mapping between optical return and mmWave reflection. The proposed framework uses LiDAR-derived geometry and return statistics to rank candidate propagation directions, while RF measurements remain responsible for final beam selection. The experimental validation is organized in three stages to separate descriptor robustness, cross-modal association, and beam-search performance. Controlled LiDAR measurements first quantify how geometric and radiometric surface descriptors vary with acquisition geometry. Matched LiDAR and 60-GHz measurements in an L-shaped corridor then determine whether these descriptors are associated with the measured surface-mediated RF response under a prescribed NLoS interaction. Finally, a separate room-scale campaign evaluates the resulting prior using exhaustive TX-RX beam maps without prescribing the underlying propagation mechanism. The measurements show that descriptor reliability depends on acquisition geometry and point-cloud representation, and that LiDAR and RF surface responses exhibit cross-modal association without supporting deterministic RF-power prediction. In the room experiment, local three-ring 3-D planarity retains a beam within 3 dB of exhaustive search at 74.5% of the measured locations while reducing RF beam-pair probing by 72% relative to exhaustive probing over the candidate search region. These results establish LiDAR-derived local surface structure as a communication-oriented prior for concentrating RF probing and reducing mmWave beam-search uncertainty.

</details>


### [255] [Power Estimation and Optimal Work-Charging Scheduling of Construction Electric Vehicles via Mobile Charging Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18494)
*Avik Ghosh,Akın Taşcıkaraoğlu,Daniela Rojas,Muhammed A. Beyazıt,Mohammad Reza Salehizadeh,Keaton Chia,Sasha Doppelt,Michael Ferry,Jan Kleissl,Sujit Dey,Yuanyuan Shi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的框架，将电动挖掘机的功率估计与移动充电感知的工作调度相结合，以优化建筑电动车的运营成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑电动车（CEVs）是柴油设备的清洁替代品，但充电基础设施稀缺、移动受限、功率消耗了解不足，阻碍了其应用。

Method: 利用真实施工示范数据，结合视频和电池遥测数据，使用约束非负最小二乘法估计子活动的平均功率；然后构建混合整数规划，联合优化工作调度、充电计划以及移动充电站（MCS）的定位和充放电。

Result: 功率模型测试误差为17% NMAE；联合优化在所有场景中实现最低运营成本，比最优基线低7%–96%，大多数实例在一小时内求解到最优。

Conclusion: 该框架有效提升了建筑电动车的经济性和实用性，数据集和代码已公开。

Abstract: Construction electric vehicles (CEVs) are a promising clean alternative to diesel-powered construction equipment, but their adoption is constrained by sparse onsite charging infrastructure, limited CEV mobility, and insufficient understanding of their power consumption. We address these gaps through a field-data-driven framework coupling CEV power estimation with mobile-charging-aware work scheduling. First, using a real-world construction demonstration at the University of California, San Diego, we develop and validate a per-subactivity power estimation model for a compact electric excavator. Manually labeled video is synchronized with coarse battery state-of-charge (SOC) telematics, and constrained nonnegative least squares is used to recover each subactivity's average power consumption. The model predicts held-out test data within $17\%$ normalized mean absolute error (NMAE), and the accompanying dataset is released publicly. Second, leveraging the subactivity power estimates, we formulate a mixed-integer program that jointly optimizes CEV work and charging schedules together with the location, timing, and charging/discharging of mobile charging stations (MCSs) serving the CEVs. The optimization accounts for energy and demand charges, carbon emissions, unmet work penalties, MCS travel, and the physical and operational constraints of the CEVs and MCSs. Across realistic scenarios drawn from the demonstration, the proposed co-optimization attains the lowest operating cost in every case, being $7$--$96\%$ below the best-performing baseline, while solving most instances to proven optimality within an hour. Dataset and scripts are available at https://github.com/ghosh-avik/CEV-MCS-Power-Estimation-and-Joint-Scheduling.

</details>


### [256] [Toward Controllability-Aware Performance Measures: A Case Study on Controllable Highway Congestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18510)
*Shreyaa Raghavan,Edgar Ramirez-Sanchez,Zhengbing He,Cathy Wu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“可控拥塞”的指标，用于量化通过限速控制可实现的交通延迟最大减少量，并利用非线性优化和模型预测控制对其进行估计。在I-24 SMART走廊数据上验证了该指标的有效性，发现可控拥塞与总延迟无直接关联，且不同操作约束对其影响不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能交通系统（ITS）能有效缓解拥堵、减少排放和事故，但缺乏标准指标来评估其改善潜力，导致基础设施部署决策可能不够精准或浪费资源。

Method: 基于METANET宏观交通模型的改进，建立非线性优化框架，并利用模型预测控制（MPC）求解可控拥塞指标。

Result: 可控拥塞与总延迟无强相关，相同旅行时间下变化幅度为13%至80%。最小限速值影响较小，而更新频率和相邻龙门架最大速度差影响显著。

Conclusion: 该指标能区分结构性不可避免的拥堵和对控制响应敏感的拥堵，帮助决策者优化ITS部署，提高成本效益。

Abstract: Advancements in emerging intelligent transportation systems (ITS) have shown immense benefit in reducing congestion, emissions, and accidents and enable lower-cost alternatives to highway lane expansion. However, there currently exists no standard metric by which agencies can assess the improvement potential from these interventions. As a result, they may fail to deploy infrastructure where it will be most promising or risk investing in infrastructure that does not meaningfully enhance performance. Our objective is to guide ITS deployment by developing a metric that quantifies the upper bound of congestion improvement from controlling speed limits on a highway. We propose controllable congestion, a metric that quantifies the maximum achievable reduction in system delay. To estimate controllable congestion, we develop a nonlinear optimization framework grounded in a reformulation of the METANET macroscopic traffic model and solved using model predictive control (MPC). Using both a synthetic scenario and highway data from the I-24 SMART Corridor in Tennessee, we find that controllable congestion is largely independent of total delay, revealing that for two days with identical travel times, controllable congestion can vary from 13\% to 80\%. We further show that the realizable share of this upper bound depends on the operational constraints imposed. On I-24, the minimum posted speed limit has little effect on controllable congestion, while frequency of speed limit update and maximum difference between adjacent gantries have large impacts. This framework allows highway operators to distinguish between congestion that is structurally unavoidable and congestion that is highly responsive to ITS, enabling more cost-effective deployment of control-based traffic infrastructure.

</details>


### [257] [Exact Robust Instability Analysis for Networked Dynamical Systems with Biological Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18553)
*Shinji Hara,Yutaka Hori,Tetsuya Iwasaki,Chung-Yao Kao,Sei Zhen Khong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了名义上不稳定的不确定网络化动态系统的鲁棒不稳定性，针对所有标称代理共享相同SISO LTI系统且受独立扰动的情况，识别了三类网络结构（循环和某些秩亏网络），将鲁棒不稳定性问题简化为单个SISO系统的分析，并精确刻画了鲁棒不稳定半径，最后应用于遗传调控网络的振荡行为分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受非线性动力学中维持周期振荡问题的启发，其精确分析通常难以处理，因此研究名义不稳定系统的鲁棒不稳定性，以提供可处理的分析框架。

Method: 通过识别三类网络结构（循环网络和某些秩亏网络），将多代理系统的鲁棒不稳定性问题约化为单个SISO系统的分析，并推导出充分条件以精确刻画鲁棒不稳定半径。

Result: 针对所识别的网络类别，给出了鲁棒不稳定半径的充分必要条件（精确刻画），并在遗传调控网络中的振荡行为分析中验证了结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，对于特定网络结构，鲁棒不稳定性分析可以简化为单系统分析，从而为分析不确定网络系统中的振荡等复杂行为提供了实用工具。

Abstract: This paper investigates robust instability in nominally unstable uncertain networked dynamical systems, where all nominal agents share an identical single-input-single-output (SISO) linear time-invariant (LTI) system and each agent is subject to independent perturbations. This setting is motivated by the problem of sustaining periodic oscillations in nonlinear dynamics, for which exact analysis is generally intractable. We identify three classes of network structures including cyclic and certain rank-deficient networks for which the robust instability problem can be reduced to the analysis of a single representative SISO system. We derive sufficient conditions that exactly characterize the robust instability radius for these network classes. Finally, we demonstrate the practical utility of the proposed results by analyzing oscillatory behavior in a genetic regulatory network.

</details>


### [258] [Robust Instability Radius for Networked Dynamical Systems: Upper and Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18561)
*Shinji Hara,Yutaka Hori,Tetsuya Iwasaki,Chung-Yao Kao,Sei Zhen Khong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究不确定网络系统的鲁棒不稳定性，定义了鲁棒不稳定半径（RIR），并给出了其上下界及在特定条件下精确刻画的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多关注网络系统的鲁棒稳定性，而对鲁棒不稳定性的刻画较少，本文旨在填补这一空白，为网络系统的安全性和稳定性分析提供理论基础。

Method: 将多智能体网络描述为对角不确定性、标称一致智能体与静态互联矩阵的反馈互联，利用小增益定理和线性矩阵不等式等工具，推导网络稳定的条件及RIR的上下界，并在秩一互联矩阵和特定符号条件下给出精确RIR。

Result: 推导出RIR的上下界，并在互联矩阵秩一且对角元同号或为零时，得到RIR的精确表达式，此时由小增益条件唯一决定。

Conclusion: 研究首次系统刻画了不确定网络系统的鲁棒不稳定半径，为理解和设计易受攻击或需保持不稳定的网络提供了新准则，未来可扩展到更一般的互联结构和动态模型。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with robust instability of uncertain network systems. We consider the multi-agent system described as a network of single-input-single-output agents with identical nominal dynamics subject to heterogeneous perturbations. The network description is formalized as a feedback interconnection of a diagonal uncertainty, nominal identical agents, and a static interconnection matrix. Assuming that the nominal network is unstable, we seek the robust instability radius (RIR), defined as the smallest norm of the stable uncertainty that renders the network stable. Conditions for the network stability are developed, and upper and lower bounds on the RIR are derived. When the network connectivity matrix is rank one and all diagonal entries share the same sign or are zero, we give conditions under which the RIR is exactly characterized by a small gain argument.

</details>


### [259] [Toward S^2C^2I-Integrated High-Altitude Platforms: Architectures, Cross-Functional Design, Evaluation, and Deployment Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18587)
*Haoxiang Luo,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文围绕高空平台（HAP）在天地一体化网络中的角色，提出统一的“感、存、通、算、智”（S^2C^2I）集成视角，系统综述了架构、关键技术、标准化与评估方法，并通过案例验证了联合编排的收益，最后展望了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAP作为中继层基础设施，其潜力远不止通信中继；随着感知、存储、计算与智能需求的融合，现有研究缺乏对HAP在S^2C^2I集成方面的统一视角和系统梳理。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，从HAP基础与角色出发，构建集成架构（多平面连通、功能拆分、云-边-HAP连续体），分类解析使能技术（射频/毫米波/太赫兹/光混合链路、感知与通感一体、机载计算、存储缓存、AI编排），并总结标准、开源资源、测试床与四级评估方法，最后通过应急响应案例进行实证。

Result: 应急响应案例表明，联合S^2C^2I编排显著提升综合服务可用性，同时减少馈电链路流量。

Conclusion: HAP正从通信中继演进为S^2C^2I一体化节点，本文提供了从平台设计到网络部署的连贯路线图，并指出了智能体AI、可信自治、语义数字孪生及可持续开放系统等未来机遇。

Abstract: High-altitude platforms (HAPs) are emerging as persistent middle-layer infrastructures for space-air-ground integrated networks (SAGINs), offering a favorable compromise among coverage, latency, endurance, and deployment flexibility. Their role, however, is evolving beyond communication relaying toward the joint provision of sensing, storage, communication, computing, and intelligence (S^2C^2I). This survey presents a unified HAP-centric perspective on S^2C^2I integration. We first review HAP fundamentals, platform categories, and their principal roles in SAGINs, including wide-area access, relaying, backhaul, edge service, low-altitude aerial coordination, and cross-layer orchestration. We then develop an integrated architecture spanning multi-plane connectivity, payload functional splits, and a cloud-edge-HAP space continuum with hierarchical data, control, computing, and storage loops. The enabling technologies are systematically examined, covering heterogeneous RF, millimeter-wave, terahertz, free-space optical, and hybrid links; sensing payloads and integrated sensing and communication; onboard computing; storage and caching; and AI-based orchestration. We further synthesize standardization progress, open software and datasets, testbeds, field evidence, and a four-level evaluation methodology ranging from component validation to mission-level effectiveness. An emergency-response case study demonstrates that joint S^2C^2I orchestration substantially improves conjunctive service availability while reducing feeder-link traffic. Finally, we identify research opportunities in agentic AI, trustworthy autonomy, goal-oriented semantic operation and digital twins, and sustainable, certifiable, and open HAP-native systems. The resulting synthesis provides a coherent roadmap from platform design to network-wide deployment.

</details>


### [260] [Payload Swing Estimation and Damping Without Payload Parameters for Multirotor UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18625)
*K. Taki,K. Umemoto*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种仅使用机载IMU和油门指令的估计与阻尼方法，无需载荷参数即可抑制多旋翼吊挂载荷的摆动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼吊挂运输灵活但会产生周期性摆动，影响跟踪并可能失稳；现有防摆方法需额外传感器或精确参数，限制实际应用。

Method: 利用扩展卡尔曼滤波器估计周期性扰动及未知摆频，并设计主动阻尼控制器在姿态环增加校正角以耗散摆动能量。

Result: 飞行实验验证了在不同缆长和质量变化下的鲁棒阻尼效果。

Conclusion: 该方法无需载荷参数即可有效抑制摆动，提升部署便利性。

Abstract: Cable-suspended payload transport by multirotor UAVs is flexible but generates periodic swing disturbance that degrades tracking and risks instability. Existing anti-swing methods require additional sensors or precise identification of cable length and payload mass, limiting field deployment. We propose a swing-estimation and damping method using only the onboard IMU and throttle command, requiring no payload parameters. An extended Kalman filter extracts the periodic disturbance with the unknown pendulum frequency as an estimated state, and an active damping controller adds a correction angle to the attitude loop to dissipate pendulum energy. Flight experiments confirm robust damping across a tested range of cable-length and mass variations.

</details>


### [261] [Detecting Feedback-path Delay Injection Attacks Using Interacting Multiple Model Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18824)
*Lovisa Eriksson,Torbjörn Wigren,Dave Zachariah,André M. H. Teixeira*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于交互多模型滤波的延迟注入攻击检测方法，在开环控制系统中能快速检测延迟攻击且误报率低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化控制系统中的恶意延迟攻击难以早期检测，需有效方案。

Method: 将延迟作为系统模式，用交互多模型滤波检测，通过理论分析简化为三参数模型并设定超参数。

Result: 在巡航控制应用中，检测延迟仅需几秒，误报概率低。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效检测延迟注入攻击，且实际可行。

Abstract: Time-delays are known to have a detrimental effect on feedback systems. In the context of networked cyber-physical systems, delays can be injected by malicious adversaries. Detecting them early is an important challenge. This paper proposes a novel variation of Interacting Multiple Model filtering to detect delay injection attacks in feedback control systems, when hidden in an open loop setting. The detection scheme is formalised by treating delay as alternative modes of the system, and theoretical analysis of the stationary distribution informs a reduction to a three parameter model as well as the choices of hyper parameter values. The method is evaluated on a cruise control application, and shows detection within a few seconds and a low false alarm probability.

</details>


### [262] [Adaptive Nonlinear Control with Online Identification and Receding-Horizon Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18717)
*Igor Ladnik*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应非线性最优控制方案，结合了滚动时域iLQR、状态估计、在线参数辨识和执行器约束。AMIGO框架将计算分为三个时间阶段：辨识、规划和闭环控制，并采用RK4进行非线性递推、LM方法进行参数优化，通过范德波尔振荡器、四旋翼和月球着陆器下降三个案例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非线性系统在动态环境和模型不确定条件下的最优控制问题，传统的离线控制方法难以适应参数变化和外部扰动，需要开发一种能够在线调整模型参数并实时生成最优控制策略的自适应控制算法。

Method: 提出了AMIGO框架，将控制过程分为三个时间阶段：参数辨识（使用Levenberg-Marquardt方法）、轨迹规划（使用滚动时域iLQR）和闭环控制。采用四阶Runge-Kutta方法（RK4）进行非线性系统动态的递推计算，并考虑了执行器约束。

Result: 通过范德波尔振荡器、四旋翼飞行器和自主月球着陆器下降三个数值案例，验证了该自适应控制方案的有效性和鲁棒性，展示了其在非线性系统中处理模型不确定性和约束的能力。

Conclusion: AMIGO方法通过在线参数辨识与滚动时域最优控制的结合，成功实现了对非线性系统在不确定环境下的自适应最优控制，为复杂动态系统的实时控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: An adaptive nonlinear optimal-control scheme is developed by combining receding-horizon iLQR, state estimation, online parameter identification, and actuator constraints. AMIGO (Adaptive Model-based Intelligent Guidance and Orchestration) organizes the computation into three Time Phases: identification, planning, and closed-loop control. The nonlinear transition is evaluated by the fourth-order Runge-Kutta method (RK4), and model parameters are refined by the Levenberg-Marquardt method (LM). The method is illustrated by a Van der Pol oscillator, a quadcopter, and an autonomous lunar-lander descent.

</details>


### [263] [Robust Joint Planning of EV and eBus Charging Infrastructure with PV Self-Consumption under Demand Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18844)
*Biswarup Mukherjee*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个混合整数线性规划框架，用于联合优化电动汽车和电动公交的充电基础设施规划，并考虑光伏自消费。模型通过鲁棒优化处理能量需求不确定性，案例研究表明车辆需求、光伏权重和V2G能力显著影响充电器部署与运行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电动汽车和电动公交的普及，充电基础设施的规划需同时考虑多种车型、运行策略的不确定性以及节点容量限制，以确保长期投资的有效性和鲁棒性。

Method: 采用混合整数线性规划（MILP）框架，先建立确定性名义模型，再扩展为基于场景的鲁棒min-max模型。通过共享第一阶段基础设施决策和场景特定运行决策，使用epigraph重构最小化基础设施成本与最坏场景运行成本之和，并引入软可行性惩罚处理能量不足和容量违规。

Result: 50节点案例研究表明，路线段电动公交需求、光伏权重改变充电器部署和技术选择，而V2G在足够大的惩罚权重下可实现硬可行性鲁棒运行。

Conclusion: 长期基础设施规划需同时考虑多样化车队、适应不同场景的运行策略以及节点级充电容量约束。

Abstract: This paper presents a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) framework for joint electric vehicle (EV) and electric bus (eBus) charging-infrastructure planning with photovoltaic (PV) self-consumption. The model co-optimizes charger siting, sizing, technology selection, eBus-to-depot assignment, and hourly charging schedules. A deterministic MILP is first formulated as a nominal benchmark and then extended to a scenario-based robust min--max formulation under vehicle energy-demand uncertainty. The robust model uses shared first-stage infrastructure decisions and scenario-specific operating decisions, and minimizes infrastructure cost plus the worst-case scenario operating cost through an epigraph reformulation. Soft-feasibility penalties quantify unmet charging energy, terminal state-of-charge (SOC) shortfall, and capacity violations under stressed scenarios. A 50-node case study shows that route-segment eBus demand and PV weighting alter charger deployment, technology mix, and grid import, while V2G enables hard-feasible robust operation at sufficiently large penalty weights. These results highlight that effective long-term infrastructure planning must simultaneously account for diverse vehicle fleets, operational strategies that adapt to different scenarios, and node-level constraints on charging capacity.

</details>


### [264] [Comparing Controller Synthesis Methods with Deadline-Miss Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18850)
*Melanie Gallant,Marc Seidel,Paolo Pazzaglia,Claudio Mandrioli,Christoph Mark,Kevin Schmidt,Frank Allgöwer,Martina Maggio*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文对实时控制系统中处理截止期超限的控制器设计方法进行了比较研究，通过定性评估和仿真案例（Furuta摆和电动机）展示了不同方法在假设、适用性和性能上的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理错过截止期的方法在假设、执行语义、运行时信息和保证方面差异显著，直接比较困难，缺乏系统性的评估工具。

Method: 采用定性评估方法，分析设计空间，识别关键假设（实时约束、控制设计标准、实现方面），并通过两个仿真案例（Furuta摆和电动机）对比不同方法在错过截止期下的实际表现。

Result: 揭示了不同方法在适用性、控制性能和保证之间的权衡，并提供了一个比较工具，便于研究人员对新方法进行系统基准测试。

Conclusion: 不同截止期感知控制方法各有优劣，选择需依据具体应用场景；所提比较工具和评估框架有助于推进该领域的研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative study of real-time controller design methods for dynamical control systems subject to deadline overruns. Numerous techniques have been proposed to handle missed deadlines, i.e., including models or information of possible deadline misses directly in the control design. These approaches substantially differ in their assumptions, supported execution semantics, required runtime information, and provided guarantees, making direct comparison challenging. We address this gap with a qualitative assessment of representative deadline-miss-aware control methods. We analyze the design space of such controllers and identify the critical assumptions on the real-time constraints, the control design criteria and implementation aspects. We expose how these different assumptions lead to the different trade-offs in applicability, and control performance and guarantees. We contribute a comparison tool enabling researchers to systematically benchmark new techniques against the state of the art. The study is supported by simulations on two case studies: a Furuta pendulum and an electric motor to illustrate the practical behavior of the different approaches under missed-deadline conditions.

</details>


### [265] [Deciding Relabeling Observation Consistency in Multi-Agent Discrete-Event Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18866)
*Tomáš Masopust*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体离散事件系统中基于公共模板的可扩展监督器在部分观测下的重标定观测一致性（ROC）的可判定性，证明其在多种情况下为PSPACE完全问题，并给出了可处理的特例、算法及充分条件，同时纠正了文献中的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展监督器通过公共模板控制同构智能体群体，部分观测下最大化许可性依赖于重标定观测一致性（ROC）条件。此前ROC的可判定性未决，且文献中存在错误的结构条件，需要澄清并修正。

Method: 通过形式化定义问题，采用计算复杂度归约证明PSPACE完全性；对可处理情况设计多项式时间算法；利用饱和、模拟等概念给出充分条件；通过反例和修正包含关系纠正文献错误。

Result: 证明了对于非确定型植物（两个可观测事件、一个不可观测事件）和确定型植物（两个可观测事件、两个不可观测事件），ROC判定为PSPACE完全；对某些特殊重标定（如不可观测事件上单射）给出多项式算法；提出基于饱和和模拟的充分条件及多项式测试；证明ROC对分量模板不相交时组合成立，若字母表也两两不相交则等价，并说明假设不可缺失；最后指出并修复了文献中的结构条件错误。

Conclusion: ROC的判定在一般情形下是PSPACE完全的，但在特殊结构和约束下可高效处理；ROC的组合性质依赖于模板和字母表的分离；修正后的伴随包含条件可有效用于保证ROC。

Abstract: Scalable supervisors for multi-agent discrete-event systems control groups of isomorphic agents through a common template. Under partial observation, such a supervisor is maximally permissive if the relabeling that maps the agents onto their template is relabeling observation consistent (ROC) and a local companion condition holds. Whether ROC is decidable was open. We show that it is PSPACE-complete: for nondeterministic plants with two observable and one unobservable event, and for deterministic plants with two observable and two unobservable events. On the tractable side, we characterize the relabelings that guarantee ROC for every plant, we give a polynomial-time algorithm for deterministic plants whose relabeling is injective on unobservable events, and we give sufficient conditions based on saturation and on simulation, with polynomial-time tests for both. We further show that ROC is compositional for components with pairwise disjoint templates, with equivalence if the alphabets are moreover pairwise disjoint, and that neither hypothesis can be dropped. Finally, we show that the structural condition proposed in the literature to guarantee ROC is incorrect, and repair the companion inclusion that the same proposition asserts.

</details>


### [266] [Limitations of RMS-based Stability Assessment of Converter-based Grids: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18989)
*Pol Jane-Soneira,Ognjen Stanojev,Francisco Canales Perez,Qian Long,Mario Schweizer,Matthias Biskoping*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文通过工业电网案例表明，基于RMS模型的特征值分析在换流器主导电网中可能错误预测稳定性，而dq坐标系动态阻抗模型能准确识别不稳定模态，与EMT仿真一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 换流器高渗透率导致工业电网出现高于传统机电范围的高频稳定性问题，而商用RMS模型忽略了电磁动态，可能无法准确评估此类电网的稳定性。

Method: 对比RMS特征值分析与EMT仿真，提出在旋转dq坐标系中保留电磁动态微分方程的动态阻抗模型，用于小信号稳定性分析。

Result: RMS模型预测稳定，但EMT仿真显示振荡发散；dq模型的特征值分析正确识别不稳定模态，与EMT结果一致。

Conclusion: RMS稳定性评估对换流器主导电网存在根本局限，dq框架动态阻抗模型为包含高频换流器-网络交互的精确小信号分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The increasing penetration of power electronic converters in industrial grids introduces stability challenges at frequencies well above the electromechanical range of traditional power systems. Commercial software tools typically perform eigenvalue-based stability analysis using root-mean-square (RMS) models that assume a quasi-stationary network, thereby neglecting electromagnetic dynamics of assets --- which is standard practice in power system analysis. This paper presents a case study of an industrial grid where the conventional RMS-based eigenvalue analysis predicts stable operation, while a detailed electromagnetic transient (EMT) simulation reveals growing oscillations, indicating instability. To bridge this gap, we introduce an alternative modeling approach that formulates the network dynamics in the rotating $dq$ reference frame, retaining the differential equations of electromagnetic dynamics. The resulting model enables eigenvalue analysis that correctly identifies the unstable modes, consistent with the EMT simulation results. Our findings highlight a fundamental limitation of RMS-based stability assessment for converter-dominated grids and demonstrate that dynamic impedance models in the dq frame provide a viable path toward accurate small-signal analysis encompassing high-frequency converter-network interactions.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [TractoGraphVLM: A Unified Vision-Language Framework for White Matter Tractography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18166)
*Gurucharan Marthi Krishna Kumar,Janine Dale Mendola,Amir Shmuel*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: TractoGraphVLM 是一个统一的图基础视觉语言模型，用于白质纤维束的分类、检索、字幕生成和视觉问答，通过共享的 GPS 图变换器和冻结的文本编码器实现多任务联合训练，并在 HCP 数据集上展示了良好的性能与零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维医学图像中的视觉语言模型已取得进展，但将其扩展到三维白质纤维束分析面临挑战，特别是纤维束的复杂拓扑结构。需要一个统一的框架来处理多种任务，并利用语言监督来增强表征。

Method: 将纤维束表示为以三维位置和切线方向为节点特征的流线图，使用 GPS 图变换器生成嵌入，通过对比学习与冻结的 BiomedBERT 文本编码器对齐，并使用带视觉前缀令牌的 BioGPT 解码器生成字幕和答案。所有任务共享一个编码器和解码器，进行联合训练。

Result: 在 HCP Young Adult 上，分类准确率 91.8%，检索 R@1 84.7%，BLEU-4=20.1，ROUGE-L=66.8，VQA 准确率 66.4%。零样本迁移到 HCP Aging 时，判别任务略有下降，生成任务下降较大。语言监督优于仅标签训练，图表示优于体素基线。

Conclusion: TractoGraphVLM 证明了单一联合训练模型可以同时完成分类、检索、描述和问答任务，并能从语言中学习可迁移的神经解剖知识，对年龄和采集偏移具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision language models have transformed 2D medical imaging, yet extending them to 3D white matter tractography remains challenging due to the complex topology of fiber bundles. We introduce TractoGraphVLM, a unified framework for four tasks, bundle classification, text-to-tract retrieval, anatomical captioning, and visual question answering, built on a shared GPS architecture, training procedure, and read-out design. Fiber bundles are represented as streamline graphs whose nodes encode 3D position and tangent orientation. A General, Powerful, Scalable (GPS) graph transformer produces bundle embeddings aligned with a frozen BiomedBERT text encoder via contrastive learning, while a BioGPT decoder with visual prefix tokens generates captions and answers. A single shared encoder and decoder is trained jointly across all four tasks and evaluated from one checkpoint. Trained on HCP Young Adult subjects, TractoGraphVLM achieves 91.8% bundle classification accuracy, 84.7% retrieval R@1, BLEU-4=20.1, ROUGE-L=66.8, and 66.4% VQA accuracy on a held-out test set. The same checkpoints transfer zero-shot to HCP Aging subjects, with a modest drop on discriminative tasks and a larger drop on generative tasks, showing robustness to age and acquisition shift. Language supervision yields richer representations than label-only training, recovering structure like hemisphere and fiber family, carried by captions but never given as a label. Swapping only the visual encoder, graphs preserving fiber orientation outperform volumetric baselines, with GPS giving the best balance. Generative metrics measure consistency with a structured knowledge base rather than independent clinical text; even so, TractoGraphVLM shows that classifying, retrieving, describing, and answering questions about a white matter bundle can be served by one jointly trained model that learns transferable neuroanatomy from language alone.

</details>


### [268] [QuARC-GS: Quantized Anchored Residual Coding for Compact Dynamic Scene Streaming with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18285)
*Vu Trung Nghia Nguyen,Yuchen Wang,Kyung Chul Lee,Kevin C. Zhou*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出QuARC-GS，一种基于量化感知的4D场景优化框架，用于在线动态场景重建，实现超高压缩同时保持重建质量和速度，存储开销较SOTA降低11倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态3D场景表示在自由视角视频流中面临存储需求大和重建/渲染速度要求高的挑战，现有方法难以支持长时间视频的在线流式传输。

Method: QuARC-GS采用单一典型帧加高压缩残差帧的结构，通过量化感知锚点变形抑制无效运动更新，以及变化门控致密化策略仅在新场景变化区域添加高斯点，减少冗余外观更新。

Result: 在多个常用数据集上，QuARC-GS在保持竞争性重建质量和训练速度的同时，每帧存储减少高达11倍。

Conclusion: QuARC-GS有效解决动态自由视角视频流中的存储和效率问题，为在线流式传输提供了可行方案。

Abstract: 3D scene representation techniques such as neural radiance fields (NeRFs) and Gaussian splatting have made substantial progress in novel view synthesis, achieving high-quality renderings from arbitrary view angles. More recently, such techniques have been extended to dynamic 3D scenes; however, achieving sustainable online free-viewpoint video (FVV) streaming remains challenging, especially for longer videos, due to significant storage demands of detailed scene representations and high reconstruction/rendering speed needs. To address these challenges, we propose Quantized Anchored Residual Coding Gaussian Streaming (QuARC-GS), a quantization-aware 4D scene optimization framework for online dynamic scene reconstruction that achieves ultra-high compression while maintaining reconstruction speed and quality. QuARC-GS represents a scene using a single canonical frame and highly compressed per-frame residuals. Specifically, we compress each residual through two complementary strategies targeting motion, appearance, and densification. We introduce quantization-aware anchor deformation, which suppresses insignificant motion updates while preserving meaningful deformations, maintaining reconstruction quality under low-storage streaming. Furthermore, we design a change-gated densification strategy that allocates new Gaussians only in regions exhibiting genuine temporal changes, effectively eliminating redundant appearance updates and reducing storage overhead. Extensive experiments on widely used datasets demonstrate that QuARC-GS enables competitive reconstruction quality and training speed while cutting per-frame storage by up to 11$\times$ compared to the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [269] [Optic Disc Segmentation in Fundus Images: From Classical Image Processing and Deformable Models to Modern AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18367)
*Buket D. Barkana*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文系统回顾了视盘分割方法从经典图像处理到现代AI的演变，总结了数据集、方法分类及核心原则，并指出当前挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视盘分割对视网膜分析和青光眼评估至关重要，但存在光照变化、病理干扰、边界模糊等挑战，且缺少系统性的方法论梳理。

Method: 采用结构化文献检索和筛选流程，将经典方法按机制分类（如强度阈值、形态学、活动轮廓等），并分析AI方法（如Transformer、边界学习、基础模型）的演进。

Result: 识别出跨代际持续的核心原则（如ROI定位、多尺度表示、几何约束），并发现边界模糊、领域偏移和跨数据集泛化仍是主要难题。

Conclusion: 视盘分割已从手工特征转向学习表示，但需进一步解决边界模糊和泛化问题，未来可借助提示分割和基础模型。

Abstract: Accurate localization and segmentation of the optic disc (OD) are important for retinal image analysis and glaucoma assessment, yet remain challenging due to variations in illumination, pathology, vascular interference, and poorly defined boundaries. This structured methodological review examines the evolution of OD segmentation from classical image-processing and deformable models to contemporary artificial intelligence (AI)-based approaches. A structured literature search and study-selection process was used to identify representative studies spanning major methodological developments. The review first summarizes commonly used fundus-image datasets, then organizes classical methods by principal mechanisms, including intensity and thresholding, histogram and entropy analysis, morphology, geometric and Hough-transform methods, filtering and feature operators, texture- and region-based approaches, and active-contour and level-set models. This paper pays particular attention to the assumptions, strengths, limitations, and complementary roles of these methods in OD localization and boundary delineation. Representative AI approaches are subsequently examined to illustrate the transition from handcrafted features and explicitly defined priors to learned representations, Transformer-based segmentation, boundary- and shape-aware learning, promptable segmentation, and retinal foundation models. Across these methodological generations, several core segmentation principles persist, including region-of-interest localization, multiscale representation, geometric and anatomical constraints, and boundary regularization, although their implementation has shifted from predefined operators to learned modules, losses, prompts, and pretrained representations. The review further identifies boundary ambiguity, anatomical variability, domain shift, and cross-dataset generalization as continuing challenges.

</details>
